## Machine Translation with LLMs

- `2026-11` **[Current state of LLMs for Arabic dialectal machine translation](https://aclanthology.org/2026.abjadnlp-1.41/)** — Jon et al. · *ABJADNLP 2026 (workshop)* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02271)]<br>Evaluates Jais, Nile, Gemma, Command-R, Mistral, Aya and GPT-4.1 on English–dialectal Arabic MT for 16 MADAR dialects, highlighting difficulty with dialectal diversity and automatic MT evaluation.
- `2026-10` **[Translation Circuit Discovery and Targeted Optimization for Low-Resource Multilingual Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2026.findings-emnlp.1348/)** — Khan et al. · *Findings of EMNLP 2026* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.21456)]<br>TITAN: finds translation circuits via causal intervention and sparse features, then preference-optimizes under 5% of parameters, raising BLEU 33.75% on Hindi-to-Urdu and 45.86% on Hindi-to-Sindhi.
- `2026-09` **[EnSiTa - A Trilingual Multi-Domain Parallel Dataset and Benchmark for Domain-Specific Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29511)** — Ranathunga et al.<br>EnSiTa: professionally translated English-Sinhala-Tamil data across seven domains plus test sets, benchmarking Transformer, NLLB-600M and Gemma 3 LLMs (1B-12B) for domain-specific MT.
- `2026-08` **[STAR : Sentence Translation Alignment Rate for Document-to-Document Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2026.findings-emnlp.699/)** — Dong et al. · *Findings of EMNLP 2026* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27161)]<br>STAR: metric for sentence-level structural fidelity in document-to-document LLM translation, plus STAR-masked Preference Optimization (StarPO), letting compact models surpass GPT-4o on news and literary text.
- `2026-08` **[Reasoning about In-Context Samples for Machine-Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2026.findings-emnlp.168/)** — Bouthors et al. · *Findings of EMNLP 2026* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27036)]<br>Fragment-based reasoning for LLM MT: the model extracts parallel source-target fragments from retrieved exemplars as reasoning traces; with Qwen3 over 6 languages it beats k-shot and drafting.
- `2026-08` **[Ladders in Chaos: When, How, (and Perhaps Why) Does Test-Time Scaling Improve LLM Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2026.findings-emnlp.2415/)** — Wu et al. · *Findings of EMNLP 2026* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28496)]<br>Compares sequential and parallel test-time scaling for LLM translation; sequential sampling has a higher ceiling and improves fluency but can degrade accuracy at large budgets.
- `2026-07` **[On the Systematic Challenges of Culturally Loaded Machine Translation: Dream of the Red Chamber as the Cultural Lens](https://aclanthology.org/2026.findings-emnlp.64/)** — Wang & Di · *Findings of EMNLP 2026* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20241)]<br>Builds a 500-segment Chinese–Japanese dataset from Dream of the Red Chamber, revealing that frontier LLMs struggle with culturally loaded content, human evaluators disagree, and automatic metrics are unreliable.
- `2026-06` **[Reasoning over Grammar: Can Synthetic Linguistic Reasoning Traces Enhance Low-Resource Machine Translation?](https://aclanthology.org/2026.findings-emnlp.281/)** — Pei et al. · *Findings of EMNLP 2026* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03782)]<br>Generates linguistic reasoning traces from UD treebanks, dictionaries and grammar rules for Xibe and Chintang MT; traces work best as inference-time guidance in in-context learning.
- `2026-06` **[Bridging Scientific Heritage: An Arabic--Russian Parallel Corpus and LLM Benchmark for Sustainable Knowledge Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30943)** — Arabov<br>Arabic-Russian scientific translation benchmark of about 27,000 sentence pairs; QLoRA-tuned Qwen2.5-7B reaches 23.15 BLEU (+4.36 over zero-shot), while three-shot prompting does not help.
- `2026-06` **[Better Literary Translation: A Multi-Aspect Data Generation and LLM Training Approach](https://aclanthology.org/2026.acl-industry.22/)** — Lin et al. · *ACL 2026 (industry)* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05924)]<br>LitMT-8B and LitMT-14B: English-to-Chinese literary translation models trained with multi-aspect generated references and GRPO, scoring 67.25 and 69.07 CEA100 on MetaphorTrans versus Claude Sonnet 4.5's 68.43.
- `2026-05` **[What Does LLM Refinement Actually Improve? A Systematic Study on Document-Level Literary Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2026.acl-long.268/)** — Tan et al. · *ACL 2026* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13368)]<br>Studies LLM self-refinement for document-level literary translation with nine LLMs and seven language pairs; document-level MT followed by segment-level refinement works best, mainly improving fluency and style.
- `2026-05` **[Research on Retrieval-Augmented Hindi-Chinese Document-Level Machine Translation for Multiple Domains](https://doi.org/10.1109/MLISE70044.2026.11607568)** — Liu · *ICML 2026*<br>Builds a five-domain Hindi-Chinese document-level translation dataset and a retrieval-augmented framework with terminology database and translation memory, fine-tuning Qwen3-8B.
- `2026-05` **[IFMTBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Multilingual Translation Instruction Following](https://aclanthology.org/2026.findings-emnlp.1016/)** — Sun et al. · *Findings of EMNLP 2026* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28218)]<br>IFMTBench: translation instruction-following benchmark in seven languages with 4,506 single- and 2,838 multi-constraint items; across 15 models, instruction following scales with size faster than translation quality.
- `2026-05` **[Beyond Literal Translation: Evaluating Cultural Effectiveness in Social Media UGC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25626)** — Wu et al. · *ICML 2026*<br>CULTURE-MT: 1,002 social media notes across 14 domains for evaluating cultural transmission and emotional resonance in translation, with fine-tuned Qwen3 baselines among 15 tested models.
- `2026-04` **[RouteLMT: Learned Sample Routing for Hybrid LLM Translation Deployment](https://aclanthology.org/2026.acl-industry.129/)** — Luo et al. · *ACL 2026 (industry)* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22520)]<br>RouteLMT: in-model router that predicts the large model's marginal gain over a small translator from prompt representations, giving better quality-budget trade-offs for hybrid LLM translation.
- `2026-04` **[One Model to Translate Them All? A Journey to Mount Doom for Multilingual Model Merging](https://aclanthology.org/2026.tacl-1.110/)** — Gain et al. · *TACL 2026* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02881)]<br>Studies weight-space merging of language models fine-tuned for bilingual MT, finding merging helps when models share a target language but degrades sharply when target languages differ.
- `2026-04` **[Beyond Reproduction: A Paired-Task Framework for Assessing LLM Comprehension and Creativity in Literary Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2026.findings-acl.2030/)** — Zhang et al. · *Findings of ACL 2026* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18169)]<br>Paired-task framework on excerpts from 11 books tests comprehension and translational creativity of 23 LLMs; strong comprehension does not yield human-level creativity, especially for English-Chinese.
- `2026-03` **[Translation Asymmetry in LLMs as a Data Augmentation Factor: A Case Study for 6 Romansh Language Varieties](https://aclanthology.org/2026.findings-emnlp.230/)** — Vamvas et al. · *Findings of EMNLP 2026* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25489)]<br>LLMs confuse Romansh varieties translating into Romansh but not out of it; synthetic data translated into German works best, beating Gemini 3 Pro by 23 BLEU in the lowest-resource variety.
- `2026-03` **[SAGE: Sustainable Agent-Guided Expert-tuning for Culturally Attuned Translation in Low-Resource Southeast Asia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19931)** — Lu et al. · *The Web Conference 2026*<br>SAGE: GRPO-trained agent curates compact data for LoRA fine-tuning of LLMs translating English and seven Southeast Asian low-resource languages, beating full-data baselines with 97.1% less data.
- `2026-03` **[Omnilingual MT: Machine Translation for 1,600 Languages](https://aclanthology.org/2026.emnlp-main.1280/)** — Alastruey et al. · *EMNLP 2026* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.16309)]<br>Omnilingual MT: MT for more than 1,600 languages via decoder-only OMT-LLaMA and encoder-decoder OMT-NLLB; 1B-8B models match or exceed a 70B LLM baseline.
- `2026-03` **[Multilingual KokoroChat: A Multi-LLM Ensemble Translation Method for Creating a Multilingual Counseling Dialogue Dataset](https://aclanthology.org/https://lrec.elra.info/lrec2026-main-695/)** — Suzuki et al. · *LREC 2026* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.22913)]<br>Multilingual KokoroChat: translates a Japanese counseling dialogue corpus into English and Chinese with a multi-LLM ensemble where one LLM fuses hypotheses from several, validated by human preference.
- `2026-03` **[Mending the Holes: Mitigating Reward Hacking in Reinforcement Learning for Multilingual Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2026.findings-acl.1682/)** — Liu et al. · *Findings of ACL 2026* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.13045)]<br>WALAR: RL with only monolingual text that patches holes in source-based QE rewards via word and language alignment; its 101-language LLM beats LLaMAX on 1400 Flores-101 directions.
- `2026-02` **[“Be My Cheese?”: Cultural Nuance Benchmarking for Machine Translation in Multilingual LLMs](https://aclanthology.org/2026.gem-main.6/)** — Doren et al. · *GEM 2026 (workshop)* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.04729)]<br>Human evaluation of cultural localization by 7 LLMs translating into 15 languages with 5 native raters each; mean quality is 1.68/3, and idioms and puns fare worst.
- `2026-02` **[Scaling Model and Data for Multilingual Machine Translation with Open Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.11961)** — Shang et al.<br>MiLMMT-46: Gemma3-based translation models built with continual pretraining and instruction finetuning while scaling model and data; covers 46 languages, competitive with Google Translate and Gemini 3 Pro.
- `2026-02` **[Scalable Multilingual Multimodal Machine Translation with Speech-Text Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.21646)** — Du et al. · *ICLR 2026*<br>Speech-guided MT framework fuses synthetic speech and text in an MLLM with a self-evolution mechanism, reporting the best results on Multi30K and on average across 108 FLORES-200 directions.
- `2026-02` **[From Utterance to Vividity: Training Expressive Subtitle Translation LLM via Adaptive Local Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.01068)** — Cui et al. · *ICLR 2026*<br>ALPO: adaptive local preference optimization for expressive subtitle translation LLMs, with a released multidirectional subtitle parallel corpus; also verifies LLMs as translation reward models and evaluators.
- `2026-01` **[MITRA: A Large-Scale Parallel Corpus and Multilingual Pretrained Language Model for Machine Translation and Semantic Retrieval for Pāli, Sanskrit, Buddhist Chinese, and Tibetan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.06400)** — Nehrdich & Keutzer<br>MITRA: mines 1.74 million Sanskrit-Chinese-Tibetan parallel sentence pairs and releases Gemma 2 MITRA models for translation into English (MITRA-MT) and semantic embedding (MITRA-E).
- `2026-01` **[HOMURA: Taming the Sand-Glass for Time-Constrained LLM Translation via Reinforcement Learning](https://aclanthology.org/2026.emnlp-main.2355/)** — Cui et al. · *EMNLP 2026* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.10187)]<br>Sand-Glass: benchmark for translation under syllable-level duration constraints, plus Homura, RL with a dynamic syllable-ratio reward that controls LLM translation length without sacrificing semantic adequacy.
- `2026-01` **[CLewR: Curriculum Learning with Restarts for Machine Translation Preference Learning](https://aclanthology.org/2026.findings-acl.1024/)** — Dragomir et al. · *Findings of ACL 2026* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.05858)]<br>CLewR: curriculum learning with restarts that repeats easy-to-hard ordering during MT preference optimization, giving consistent gains across Gemma2, Qwen2.5 and Llama3.1.
- `2026-01` **[Benchmarking Machine Translation on Chinese Social Media Texts](https://aclanthology.org/2026.findings-emnlp.386/)** — Zhao et al. · *Findings of EMNLP 2026* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.22931)]<br>CSM-MTBench: Chinese social-media MT benchmark over five directions with slang-heavy and style-driven subsets, scored by slang success rate and embedding plus LLM-judge metrics on 20+ models.
- `2026` **[Unlocking Latent Discourse Translation in LLMs Through Quality-Aware Decoding](https://aclanthology.org/2026.eacl-long.220/)** — Mohammed et al. · *EACL 2026*<br>Shows LLMs encode discourse knowledge for context-aware translation and that quality-aware (minimum Bayes risk) decoding extracts it better than other decoding methods, aligning closer with human preferences.
- `2026` **[TranslatePsy-AfriSLM: High-Quality Data Scaling For Low-Resource Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2026.emnlp-main.1686/)** — Gritta et al. · *EMNLP 2026* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18655)]<br>TranslatePsy-AfriSLM: open MT resources for 19 Sub-Saharan African languages; QE filtering removes up to 96% of training tokens without quality loss, and 0.8B models beat TranslateGemma-27B.
- `2026` **[Towards Singable Lyrics Translation Using Large Language Models](https://aclanthology.org/2026.eacl-srw.42/)** — Hanze et al. · *EACL 2026 (SRW)*<br>Curates a lyrics translation dataset over six directions among Chinese, Japanese and English; multi-prompt LLM strategies improve rhythmic alignment and phonological naturalness over naive translation.
- `2026` **[Toward End-to-End Open-Weight LLM-RBMT for Endangered Languages](https://aclanthology.org/2026.emnlp-main.1832/)** — Coleman & Krishnamachari · *EMNLP 2026*<br>Open-weight LLM-assisted rule-based MT for Owens Valley Paiute: Qwen2.5-3B LoRA adapters trained on data synthesized from the language specification match gpt-4o-mini (COMET-c 0.472 vs. 0.448).
- `2026` **[Test-Time Scaling of Reasoning Models for Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2026.eacl-long.133/)** — Li et al. · *EACL 2026*<br>Evaluates 12 reasoning models on MT benchmarks: test-time scaling gives limited gains for direct translation, helps after domain-specific fine-tuning, hurts when reasoning is forced, and works well for post-editing.
- `2026` **[Syntax as a Rosetta Stone: Universal Dependencies for In-Context Coptic Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2026.findings-acl.1803/)** — Purushothama et al. · *Findings of ACL 2026*<br>Improves LLM in-context translation of Coptic by adding Universal Dependencies parses to prompts; syntax alone helps less than dictionary glosses, but combining both gives significant gains across model sizes.
- `2026` **[Steering Large Language Models for Machine Translation Personalization](https://aclanthology.org/2026.eacl-long.217/)** — Scalena et al. · *EACL 2026* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.16612)]<br>Explores prompting and contrastive SAE steering to personalize LLM literary translation toward a translator's style from few examples; SAE steering gives robust style conditioning more efficiently than prompting.
- `2026` **[SrcMix: Mixing of Related Source Languages Benefits Extremely Low-resource Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2026.findings-eacl.332/)** — Kumar et al. · *Findings of EACL 2026*<br>SrcMix: source-side mixing of related extremely low-resource languages during fine-tuning beats target-side mixing by up to +12 BLEU; releases the first Angika MT dataset and compares Aya-101 with mT5.
- `2026` **[R1-T1: Fully Incentivizing Translation Capability in LLMs via Reasoning Learning](https://aclanthology.org/2026.tacl-1.95/)** — He et al. · *TACL 2026* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.19735)]<br>R1-T1: RL with six expert-curated, human-aligned CoT patterns for reasoning-based MT, improving quality across 10+ languages and 40+ directions on Flores-101 and four domain tasks.
- `2026` **[One Pair Suffices: Unlocking Universal Zero-Shot Translation via Cross-Architecture Alignment](https://aclanthology.org/2026.acl-long.1912/)** — Zong et al. · *ACL 2026*<br>HCA: fuses a frozen NLLB encoder with a Qwen decoder via adapters; training only on German-English unlocks zero-shot X-to-English translation for dozens of languages, recovering over 96.7% of all-pair performance.
- `2026` **[NiuTrans.LMT: Toward Inclusive and Scalable Multilingual Machine Translation with LLMs](https://aclanthology.org/2026.acl-long.1153/)** — Luo et al. · *ACL 2026* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.07003)]<br>NiuTrans.LMT: Chinese-English-centric translation models (0.6B–8B) covering 60 languages and 234 directions; identifies Directional Degeneration in multi-way SFT and mitigates it with Strategic Downsampling.
- `2026` **[MeRP: Efficient Multi-Expert Routing Prompt Encoding for Multilingual Translation with LLMs](https://aclanthology.org/2026.emnlp-main.2006/)** — Fan et al. · *EMNLP 2026*<br>MeRP: multi-expert routing prompt encoding for parameter-efficient multilingual MT tuning that picks experts by translation direction, updating 0.06% of parameters and improving five language pairs.
- `2026` **[ManCC: A Task-Anchored Benchmark for Manchu–Classical Chinese Cross-Lingual Modeling](https://aclanthology.org/2026.findings-acl.1359/)** — Wang et al. · *Findings of ACL 2026*<br>ManCC: 16,627-pair Manchu–Classical Chinese parallel corpus with human evaluation protocol; across non-pretrained, multilingual pretrained and LLM families, broader pretraining language coverage aids low-resource transfer.
- `2026` **[Machine Translation for Low-Resource Languages through Monolingual Data and LLM: A Case Study of English-to-Basque](https://aclanthology.org/2026.eacl-srw.6/)** — Luu et al. · *EACL 2026 (SRW)*<br>Trains an English-to-Basque LLM MT system on back-translated Basque monolingual data with SFT then DPO; SFT clearly improves quality while DPO adds only marginal gains.
- `2026` **[M^2PO: Multi-Perspective Multi-Pair Preference Optimization for Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2026.acl-long.469/)** — Wang et al. · *ACL 2026* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.13434)]<br>M^2PO: MT preference optimization decoupling fidelity from fluency with multi-pair objectives over full candidate lists; a 9B model reaches parity with GPT-4o and Gemini-2.0-Flash.
- `2026` **[MT^{3}: A Synergistic Multi-Task RL Framework for Specializing MLLMs in Text Image Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2026.acl-long.460/)** — Feng et al. · *ACL 2026*<br>MT^3: multi-task RL framework that trains MLLMs on text recognition, context-aware reasoning and translation for end-to-end text image MT; introduces XHSPost, a social-media TIMT benchmark.
- `2026` **[LLM Reasoning for Machine Translation: Synthetic Data Generation over Thinking Tokens](https://aclanthology.org/2026.emnlp-main.1302/)** — Zebaze et al. · *EMNLP 2026*<br>Finds general reasoning models' thinking tokens do not help MT and distilled human-style CoT does not beat standard fine-tuning; gains come from teacher translation attempts in traces.
- `2026` **[Incentivizing Parametric Knowledge via Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards for Cross-Cultural Entity Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2026.acl-long.254/)** — Zhou et al. · *ACL 2026* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16881)]<br>EA-RLVR: entity-anchored RL with verifiable rewards for cross-cultural entity translation; 7k samples raise Qwen3-14B entity accuracy from 23.66% to 31.87% and add +1.35 XCOMET on WMT24pp.
- `2026` **[Idioms Understood, Yet Translated Literally: Diagnosing Literal Translation Bias in Multilingual LLMs](https://aclanthology.org/2026.emnlp-main.2619/)** — Sun et al. · *EMNLP 2026*<br>IdiomDIT: diagnoses idiom detection, interpretation and translation across six language pairs and five models; literal translation bias persists, while hidden-state probe evidence collapses under leakage controls.
- `2026` **[IPA-Verified Singable Lyrics Translation with LLM Agents](https://aclanthology.org/2026.findings-emnlp.1174/)** — Chiu et al. · *Findings of EMNLP 2026*<br>LLM agent translates lyrics while calling IPA-based skills to verify syllable counts and rhyme, improving constraint satisfaction over a supervised baseline and transferring to Spanish and Japanese.
- `2026` **[GRRM: Group Relative Reward Modeling for Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2026.emnlp-main.2283/)** — Yang et al. · *EMNLP 2026* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.14028)]<br>GRRM: group relative reward model that jointly ranks all translation candidates for GRPO, improving MT quality and unlocking reasoning comparable to strong reasoning models.
- `2026` **[G-IdiomAlign: A Gloss-Pivoted Benchmark for Cross-Lingual Idiom Alignment](https://aclanthology.org/2026.acl-long.1794/)** — Ye et al. · *ACL 2026* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18989)]<br>G-IdiomAlign: Wiktionary gloss-anchored benchmark for cross-lingual idiom alignment; literal translation is a dominant LLM failure mode, especially into low-resource languages, and glosses consistently help but performance stays modest.
- `2026` **[Extracting Small Translation Specialists from LLMs by Aggressively Pruning Experts](https://aclanthology.org/2026.emnlp-main.2638/)** — Martin et al. · *EMNLP 2026* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28042)]<br>Prunes translation-irrelevant experts from MoE LLMs without retraining, removing half of experts with negligible MT degradation and 75% with a short SFT while recovering baseline quality.
- `2026` **[Every Word Presented in Context: Syntactic Coverage as Objective for Low-Resource Machine Translation with Large Language Models](https://aclanthology.org/https://lrec.elra.info/lrec2026-main-694/)** — Frontull & Ströhle · *LREC 2026*<br>Fragment-Shot Prompting: retrieves examples for every word of a sentence for LLM translation between Italian and two Ladin variants, giving the best results especially into Ladin.
- `2026` **[ETHICA-MT: Introducing a Framework and Dataset for Studying Ethical Orientations in LLM-based Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2026.findings-acl.1098/)** — Asscher et al. · *Findings of ACL 2026*<br>ETHICA-MT Bench: six-language benchmark of ethical conflicts in translation; LLMs default to faithful representation of the source and vary in implementing specified ethical approaches.
- `2026` **[Culture-Aware Machine Translation in Large Language Models: Benchmarking and Investigation](https://aclanthology.org/2026.acl-long.1367/)** — Yuan et al. · *ACL 2026* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24361)]<br>CanMT: culture-aware parallel MT dataset drawn from novels, with an evaluation framework; finds a persistent gap between LLMs recognizing culture-specific items and translating them correctly.
- `2026` **[Cultural and Knowledge Biases in LLMs through the Lens of Entity-Aware Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/https://lrec.elra.info/lrec2026-main-692/)** — Xu et al. · *LREC 2026*<br>Classifies entities as culturally agnostic, sensitive or local; across 11 LLMs and 10 languages, external knowledge helps culturally local entities substantially more than agnostic ones (up to 70% in m-ETA).
- `2026` **[Cross-Preference Learning for Sentence-Level and Context-Aware Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2026.emnlp-main.2543/)** — Li et al. · *EMNLP 2026*<br>Cross-Preference Learning: preference optimization with intra- and cross-condition preferences between sentence-level and context-aware MT, improving quality and robustness on Qwen3 and Llama-3 models.
- `2026` **[ContextRouter: Learning When to Use Context for Efficient LLM Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2026.emnlp-main.1635/)** — Choudhary et al. · *EMNLP 2026*<br>ContextRouter: lightweight router choosing per segment whether an LLM translator uses no context, previous context or refinement, improving quality-cost tradeoffs on TED, QED and OpenSubtitles.
- `2026` **[Completely Modular Fine-tuning for Dynamic Language Adaptation](https://aclanthology.org/2026.findings-eacl.252/)** — Cao et al. · *Findings of EACL 2026*<br>Trains English-centric input and output LoRA adapters per language and merges them for any-direction LLM translation, reaching up to 86% of full multi-parallel fine-tuning with 0.1% parameters.
- `2026` **[CoPiT: Cognitive Pivot Translation for Digraphic Low-Resource Mongolian in the Traditional Script](https://aclanthology.org/2026.emnlp-main.2685/)** — Bayarsaikhan et al. · *EMNLP 2026*<br>CoPiT: routes Traditional-script Mongolian translation through Cyrillic script after resolving script ambiguity, raising BLEU and up to 1.5-1.6x COMET so open models match GPT-4.1.
- `2026` **[Author-Stigmatising Hazards in LLM-Generated Literary Translation: Evaluation and Mitigation through Danmei-EPV](https://aclanthology.org/2026.findings-emnlp.512/)** — Zhang & Bhattacharjee · *Findings of EMNLP 2026*<br>Danmei-EPV: 412-item benchmark of author-stigmatising hazards in LLM literary translation; all eight LLMs show voice collapse, connotation flattening and hedging erasure at 5 to 6× reference rates.
- `2026` **[Assessing the Impact of Typological Features on Multilingual Machine Translation in the Age of Large Language Models](https://aclanthology.org/2026.eacl-long.109/)** — Hirak et al. · *EACL 2026* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.03551)]<br>Finds target-language typology drives translation quality of NLLB-200 and Tower+ even after controlling for resources and script, and releases typological properties for 212 FLORES+ languages.
- `2026` **[Are Small Language Models the Silver Bullet to Low-Resource Languages Machine Translation?](https://aclanthology.org/2026.loresmt-1.1/)** — Song et al. · *LORESMT 2026 (workshop)*<br>Evaluates small language models on translation across 200 languages; distillation from strong teachers using mostly monolingual low-resource data often lets 2B–3B models match or surpass systems up to 70B.
- `2025-11` **[LangMark: A Multilingual Dataset for Automatic Post-Editing](https://aclanthology.org/2025.acl-long.1569/)** — Velazquez et al. · *ACL 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.17153)]<br>LangMark: 206,983 human post-edited NMT triplets from English into seven languages; few-shot prompted LLMs perform automatic post-editing that improves on leading commercial MT systems.
- `2025-11` **[Discourse Graph Guided Document Translation with Large Language Models](https://aclanthology.org/2026.eacl-long.75/)** — Pham et al. · *EACL 2026* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.07230)]<br>TransGraph: conditions each document-translation chunk on relevant neighbours in a discourse graph; beats baselines on three document-level MT benchmarks in six languages with lower token cost.
- `2025-09` **[Liaozhai through the Looking-Glass: On Paratextual Explicitation of Culture-Bound Terms in Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2025.emnlp-main.1744/)** — Shen et al. · *EMNLP 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.23395)]<br>Introduces paratextual explicitation for MT with 560 expert-aligned paratexts from four English Liaozhai translations; LLM-generated paratexts aid comprehension but trail translator-authored ones.
- `2025-08` **[TopXGen: Topic-Diverse Parallel Data Generation for Low-Resource Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2025.findings-emnlp.1217/)** — Zebaze et al. · *Findings of EMNLP 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.08680)]<br>TopXGen: uses LLMs to generate topic-diverse text in low-resource languages, then backtranslates it into parallel data for in-context learning and fine-tuning of low-resource MT.
- `2025-08` **[Evaluating LLMs on Chinese Idiom Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.10421)** — Yang et al. · *COLM 2025*<br>IdiomEval: error taxonomy and 900 annotated Chinese idiom translations from nine systems; GPT-4 errs in 28% of cases and existing metrics correlate below 0.48 with humans.
- `2025-08` **[DocHPLT: A Massively Multilingual Document-Level Translation Dataset](https://aclanthology.org/2025.wmt-1.17/)** — O'Brien et al. · *WMT 2025 (workshop)* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.13079)]<br>DocHPLT: 124 million aligned document pairs across 50 languages paired with English (4.26 billion sentences); LLMs fine-tuned on it outperform instruction-tuned baselines, especially for under-resourced languages.
- `2025-07` **[Towards Style Alignment in Cross-Cultural Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2025.acl-long.1550/)** — Havaldar et al. · *ACL 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.00216)]<br>Shows LLM translations bias style toward neutrality and do worse for non-Western languages; proposes RASTA, retrieval-augmented stylistic alignment to convey cultural communication norms.
- `2025-07` **[Single-to-mix Modality Alignment with Multimodal Large Language Model for Document Image Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2025.acl-long.606/)** — Liang et al. · *ACL 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.07572)]<br>M4Doc: aligns an image-only encoder with an MLLM's multimodal representations so a lightweight document image MT model gains visual-textual knowledge, bypassing the MLLM at inference.
- `2025-07` **[Seed-X: Building Strong Multilingual Translation LLM with 7B Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.13618)** — Cheng et al.<br>Seed-X: open 7B instruct and reasoning translation LLMs pretrained on monolingual and bilingual data in 28 languages, with CoT fine-tuning and RL, comparable to Gemini-2.5 and GPT-4o.
- `2025-07` **[Improving MLLM’s Document Image Machine Translation via Synchronously Self-reviewing Its OCR Proficiency](https://aclanthology.org/2025.findings-acl.1213/)** — Liang et al. · *Findings of ACL 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.08309)]<br>SSR: fine-tuning that has MLLMs generate OCR text before translation for document image MT, mitigating forgetting of OCR ability and improving both OCR and translation generalization.
- `2025-07` **[How Important is ‘Perfect’ English for Machine Translation Prompts?](https://aclanthology.org/2026.findings-eacl.38/)** — Schmidtová et al. · *Findings of EACL 2026* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.09509)]<br>Evaluates how plausible and synthetic prompt errors affect LLM translation and MT evaluation; prompt quality strongly affects translation, mainly via instruction following, and character-level noise hurts more than phrasal perturbations.
- `2025-07` **[Advancing Dialectal Arabic to Modern Standard Arabic Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.20301)** — Alabdullah et al.<br>Evaluates prompting in six LLMs and a resource-efficient fine-tuning pipeline for Levantine, Egyptian and Gulf Arabic to MSA translation; fine-tuned Gemma2-9B beats zero-shot GPT-4o.
- `2025-06` **[Exploring In-context Example Generation for Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2025.findings-acl.1362/)** — Lee et al. · *Findings of ACL 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.00507)]<br>DAT: generates relevant, diverse in-context translation examples without external resources, improving LLM translation for low-resource languages lacking human-annotated demonstration pools.
- `2025-05` **[Tulun: Transparent and Adaptable Low-resource Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2025.acl-demo.13/)** — Merx et al. · *ACL 2025 (demo)* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.18683)]<br>Tulun: open-source platform combining neural MT with glossary- and translation-memory-guided LLM post-editing, gaining 16.90-22.41 ChrF++ on medical and disaster relief translation for Tetun and Bislama.
- `2025-05` **[Scaling Low-Resource MT via Synthetic Data Generation with LLMs](https://aclanthology.org/2025.emnlp-main.1408/)** — Gibert et al. · *EMNLP 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.14423)]<br>Builds an LLM-generated document-level synthetic corpus from English Europarl for seven languages, pivoted to 147 more pairs, and releases SynOPUS; synthetic data improves low-resource MT.
- `2025-05` **[Combining the Best of Both Worlds: A Method for Hybrid NMT and LLM Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2025.findings-acl.266/)** — Wu et al. · *Findings of ACL 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.13554)]<br>Proposes a decider that routes sentences between NMT and LLM translation using source features, reaching optimal translation quality with minimal LLM usage on multilingual test sets.
- `2025-04` **[Two Intermediate Translations Are Better Than One: Fine-tuning LLMs for Document-level Translation Refinement](https://aclanthology.org/2025.acl-long.726/)** — Dong et al. · *ACL 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.05614)]<br>Fine-tunes LLMs to refine document-level translations from both sentence-level and document-level drafts, with quality-aware weighting toward harder cases; tested on ten tasks.
- `2025-04` **[TRANS-ZERO: Self-Play Incentivizes Large Language Models for Multilingual Translation Without Parallel Data](https://aclanthology.org/2025.findings-acl.637/)** — Zou et al. · *Findings of ACL 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.14669)]<br>TRANS-ZERO: self-play with Genetic Monte-Carlo Tree Search and preference optimization trains LLMs for multilingual MT from monolingual data only, matching supervised parallel-data training.
- `2025-04` **[Multilingual Contextualization of Large Language Models for Document-Level Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.12140)** — Ramos et al. · *COLM 2025*<br>DocBlocks: curated document-level data for fine-tuning LLMs on several translation paradigms (document-to-document and chunk-level), improving document translation quality and speed over prompting and agent methods.
- `2025-04` **[MT-R1-Zero: Advancing LLM-based Machine Translation via R1-Zero-like Reinforcement Learning](https://aclanthology.org/2025.findings-emnlp.1015/)** — Feng et al. · *Findings of EMNLP 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.10160)]<br>MT-R1-Zero: R1-Zero-style RL for MT without SFT, using rule-metric mixed rewards; the 7B model averages 62.25, on par with GPT-4o and Claude-3.5-Sonnet.
- `2025-04` **[LLMs Can Achieve High-quality Simultaneous Machine Translation as Efficiently as Offline](https://aclanthology.org/2025.findings-acl.1045/)** — Fu et al. · *Findings of ACL 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.09570)]<br>Trains LLMs for simultaneous MT by interleaving source and target tokens with latency-specific special tokens in SFT data, preserving offline translation and generalizing to document-level SiMT.
- `2025-04` **[DMDTEval: An Evaluation and Analysis of LLMs on Disambiguation in Multi-domain Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2025.emnlp-main.309/)** — Man et al. · *EMNLP 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.20371)]<br>DMDTEval: test set with multi-domain ambiguous word annotations, prompt strategies and disambiguation metrics to evaluate LLM translation across 4 language pairs and 13 domains.
- `2025-04` **[Calibrating Translation Decoding with Quality Estimation on LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.19044)** — Wu et al. · *NeurIPS 2025*<br>Calibrates LLM translation likelihoods to quality by optimizing their Pearson correlation with 2K instances per direction, improving even Tower, and the calibrated likelihood serves as a quality-estimation proxy.
- `2025-03` **[SwiLTra-Bench: The Swiss Legal Translation Benchmark](https://aclanthology.org/2025.acl-long.725/)** — Niklaus et al. · *ACL 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.01372)]<br>SwiLTra-Bench: 180K+ aligned Swiss legal translation pairs across all Swiss languages and English; frontier LLMs lead across document types, fine-tuned open SLMs still trail them; adds SwiLTra-Judge.
- `2025-03` **[Source-primed Multi-turn Conversation Helps Large Language Models Translate Documents](https://aclanthology.org/2025.findings-emnlp.1289/)** — Hu et al. · *Findings of EMNLP 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.10494)]<br>Translates documents segment by segment in a multi-turn LLM conversation, optionally priming with the whole source document, outperforming single-turn and independent-segment translation without training.
- `2025-03` **[Preserving Cultural Identity with Context-Aware Translation Through Multi-Agent AI Systems](https://aclanthology.org/2025.lm4uc-1.7/)** — Anik et al. · *LM4UC 2025 (workshop)* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.04827)]<br>Multi-agent framework with translation, interpretation, content synthesis and bias-evaluation agents for culturally adaptive translation in underserved languages, reported to outperform GPT-4o.
- `2025-03` **[MT-RewardTree: A Comprehensive Framework for Advancing LLM-Based Machine Translation via Reward Modeling](https://aclanthology.org/2025.findings-emnlp.1007/)** — Feng et al. · *Findings of EMNLP 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.12123)]<br>MT-RewardTree: builds token-level MT preference pairs via approximate MCTS, an MT-specific reward-model benchmark and process reward models that enable test-time alignment and better hypothesis ensembling.
- `2025-03` **[Lost in Literalism: How Supervised Training Shapes Translationese in LLMs](https://aclanthology.org/2025.acl-long.630/)** — Li et al. · *ACL 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.04369)]<br>Traces translationese in LLM translations to supervised fine-tuning biases and reduces it by polishing golden references and filtering unnatural training instances.
- `2025-03` **[Is Small Language Model the Silver Bullet to Low-Resource Languages Machine Translation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.24102)** — Song et al.<br>Evaluates smaller LLMs on FLORES-200 across 200 languages and distills large teachers into small models; Llama-3.2-3B English-Luxembourgish LLM-judge score rises from 0.36 to 0.89.
- `2025-03` **[Compositional Translation: A Novel LLM-based Approach for Low-resource Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2025.findings-emnlp.1216/)** — Zebaze et al. · *Findings of EMNLP 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.04554)]<br>Compositional translation: an LLM splits a sentence into phrases, translates each with retrieved demonstrations, then the full sentence, improving results on FLORES 200, NTREX 128 and TICO-19.
- `2025-03` **[Beyond Decoder-only: Large Language Models Can be Good Encoders for Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2025.findings-acl.490/)** — Luo et al. · *Findings of ACL 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.06594)]<br>Uses LLMs as NMT encoders with an unchanged NMT decoder, matching or surpassing baselines on WMT with 2.4-6.5x inference speedups and 75% smaller KV cache.
- `2025-02` **[WMT24++: Expanding the Language Coverage of WMT24 to 55 Languages & Dialects](https://aclanthology.org/2025.findings-acl.634/)** — Deutsch et al. · *Findings of ACL 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.12404)]<br>WMT24++: extends WMT24 to 55 languages and dialects with human references and post-edits in four domains; automatic metrics rank LLMs the best MT systems in all 55 languages.
- `2025-02` **[Understanding In-Context Machine Translation for Low-Resource Languages: A Case Study on Manchu](https://aclanthology.org/2025.acl-long.429/)** — Pei et al. · *ACL 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.11862)]<br>Tests which resources aid LLM in-context MT for Manchu, using enciphered text to remove prior knowledge; dictionaries and parallel examples help, grammars hardly do.
- `2025-02` **[Science Across Languages: Assessing LLM Multilingual Translation of Scientific Papers](https://aclanthology.org/2026.findings-eacl.204/)** — Kleidermacher & Zou · *Findings of EACL 2026* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.17882)]<br>Translates scientific articles into 28 languages with LLMs while preserving JATS XML, evaluated with a QA-based benchmark (95.9% average) and a user study with 15 researchers.
- `2025-02` **[SMOL: Professionally Translated Parallel Data for 115 Under-represented Languages](https://aclanthology.org/2025.wmt-1.85/)** — Caswell et al. · *WMT 2025 (workshop)* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.12301)]<br>SMOL: professionally translated sentence- and document-level data for 124 under-resourced languages (6.1M tokens); prompting or fine-tuning LLMs with it improves chrF; includes factuality ratings.
- `2025-02` **[Multilingual Machine Translation with Open Large Language Models at Practical Scale: An Empirical Study](https://aclanthology.org/2025.naacl-long.280/)** — Cui et al. · *NAACL 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.02481)]<br>Evaluates six open LLMs under 10B parameters on multilingual MT and introduces GemmaX2-28, a 9B model trained with Parallel-First Monolingual-Second data mixing for 28 languages.
- `2025-02` **[Evaluating o1-Like LLMs: Unlocking Reasoning for Translation through Comprehensive Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.11544)** — Chen et al.<br>Evaluates o1-like LLMs on multilingual MT: DeepSeek-R1 beats GPT-4o on contextless tasks, quality grows with model size and lower temperature, but Chinese-centric outputs tend to ramble.
- `2025-02` **[Can Uniform Meaning Representation Help GPT-4 Translate from Indigenous Languages?](https://aclanthology.org/2025.acl-short.23/)** — Wein · *ACL 2025 (short)* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.08900)]<br>Adds Uniform Meaning Representation annotations to GPT-4 prompts for translating Navajo, Arapaho and Kukama; UMR yields statistically significant gains in most test cases.
- `2025-02` **[Automatic Input Rewriting Improves Translation with Large Language Models](https://aclanthology.org/2025.naacl-long.542/)** — Ki & Carpuat · *NAACL 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.16682)]<br>Tests 21 LLM input-rewriting methods with 3 open-weight LLMs for English into 6 languages; simplification is the most effective rewrite, further improved with quality-estimation-based translatability checks.
- `2025-02` **[Asymmetric Conflict and Synergy in Post-training for LLM-based Multilingual Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2025.findings-acl.944/)** — Zheng et al. · *Findings of ACL 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.11223)]<br>Finds asymmetric linguistic conflict and synergy across directions in LLM multilingual MT post-training; direction-aware training with group-wise merging nears X-ALMA-13B using 5.5x fewer pretraining tokens.
- `2025-01` **[Proverbs Run in Pairs: Evaluating Proverb Translation Capability of Large Language Model](https://aclanthology.org/2025.findings-acl.83/)** — Wang et al. · *Findings of ACL 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.11953)]<br>Builds a proverb translation dataset, standalone and in conversation, for four language pairs; LLMs generally beat NMT, and BLEU, CHRF++ and COMET prove inadequate for proverbs.
- `2025-01` **[Mitigating Hallucinated Translations in Large Language Models with Hallucination-focused Preference Optimization](https://aclanthology.org/2025.naacl-long.175/)** — Tang et al. · *NAACL 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.17295)]<br>Hallucination-focused preference datasets for fine-tuning LLM translators cut hallucinations by 96% on average across five language pairs and 89% in three unseen target languages.
- `2025-01` **[CRPO: Confidence-Reward Driven Preference Optimization for Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2025.findings-acl.31/)** — Cui et al. · *Findings of ACL 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.13927)]<br>CRPO: selects DPO preference data for MT by combining reward scores with model confidence, outperforming RS-DPO, RSO and MBR score in accuracy and data efficiency, and works for NLLB.
- `2025-01` **[Adaptive Few-shot Prompting for Machine Translation with Pre-trained Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.01679)** — Tang et al. · *AAAI 2025*<br>AFSP: retrieves semantically similar translation demonstrations using the deployed LLM's own embeddings to build adaptive few-shot prompts for LLM-based MT.
- `2025-01` **[AFRIDOC-MT: Document-level MT Corpus for African Languages](https://aclanthology.org/2025.emnlp-main.1413/)** — Alabi et al. · *EMNLP 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.06374)]<br>AFRIDOC-MT: document-level multi-parallel health and IT news corpus for English and five African languages; GPT-4o leads general LLMs, and some LLMs show off-target translation and repetition.
- `2025` **[Translating Movie Subtitles by Large Language Models using Movie-meta Information](https://aclanthology.org/2025.acl-srw.22/)** — Pramodya et al. · *ACL 2025 (SRW)*<br>Builds a multilingual dataset aligning OpenSubtitles with Wikipedia articles; adding movie meta-information to prompts improves GPT-3.5, GPT-4o and LLaMA-3 subtitle translation accuracy.
- `2025` **[Translating Domain-Specific Terminology in Typologically-Diverse Languages: A Study in Tax and Financial Education](https://aclanthology.org/2025.emnlp-main.1774/)** — Oncevay et al. · *EMNLP 2025*<br>Gold-standard tax and financial-education terminology resource in seven typologically diverse languages (incl. Haitian Creole) with over 3,000 annotated terms; MT systems and LLMs struggle with specialized tax terms.
- `2025` **[Toward Low-Resource Languages Machine Translation: A Language-Specific Fine-Tuning With LoRA for Specialized Large Language Models](https://doi.org/10.1109/ACCESS.2025.3549795)** — Liang et al. · *IEEE Access 2025*<br>LSFTL: language-specific LoRA fine-tuning of LLM attention and feed-forward layers for low-resource Asian MT, improving COMET by 1-3 points over specialized multilingual MT models.
- `2025` **[Toward Global AI Inclusivity: A Large-Scale Multilingual Terminology Dataset (GIST)](https://aclanthology.org/2025.findings-acl.1148/)** — Liu et al. · *Findings of ACL 2025*<br>GIST: 5K AI terms translated into Arabic, Chinese, French, Japanese and Russian via LLM extraction plus human translation; LLM-prompted post-translation refinement improves BLEU and COMET.
- `2025` **[The Impact of Domain-Specific Terminology on Machine Translation for Finance in European Languages](https://aclanthology.org/2025.naacl-long.140/)** — Oncevay et al. · *NAACL 2025*<br>Builds a 22-language multi-parallel European Central Bank corpus and English financial glossary to compare multilingual MT systems with LLMs; translation quality correlates with financial term accuracy.
- `2025` **[Reasoning for Translation: Comparative Analysis of Chain-of-Thought and Tree-of-Thought Prompting for LLM Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2025.acl-srw.17/)** — Nguyen & Xu · *ACL 2025 (SRW)*<br>Compares zero-shot, few-shot, chain-of-thought and tree-of-thought prompting for LLM translation across language pairs and domains, finding large variation with direction-specific gains.
- `2025` **[MLAS-LoRA: Language-Aware Parameters Detection and LoRA-Based Knowledge Transfer for Multilingual Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2025.acl-long.762/)** — Dong et al. · *ACL 2025*<br>MLAS-LoRA: transfers language-general and language-specific knowledge from a large teacher LLM to a student via multiple language-aware LoRA modules, reducing parameter interference and gaining +1.7 BLEU on average.
- `2025` **[Large Language Models for Persian-English Idiom Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2025.naacl-long.405/)** — Rezaeimanesh et al. · *NAACL 2025*<br>Introduces Persian-English idiom translation datasets drawn from PersianIdioms (2,200 idioms); Claude-3.5-Sonnet performs best, and pairing weaker LLMs with Google Translate helps English-to-Persian.
- `2025` **[InImageTrans: Multimodal LLM-based Text Image Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2025.findings-acl.1039/)** — Zuo et al. · *Findings of ACL 2025*<br>InImageTrans: multimodal LLM for text image MT trained with multi-conditional DPO against repetition and omission hallucinations, plus the MCiT benchmark.
- `2025` **[Glitter: A Multi-Sentence, Multi-Reference Benchmark for Gender-Fair German Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2025.findings-emnlp.1002/)** — Pranav et al. · *Findings of EMNLP 2025*<br>Glitter: English-German benchmark of extended passages with professional gender-fair translations in three forms; language models default to masculine generics even when told to translate inclusively.
- `2025` **[Exploring Context Strategies in LLMs for Discourse-Aware Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2025.findings-emnlp.1324/)** — Choudhary et al. · *Findings of EMNLP 2025*<br>Studies how prior source sentences, model hypotheses and references as context affect LLM translation and discourse phenomena; combining source with the model's own prior hypotheses improves consistency.
- `2025` **[Enhancing Machine Translation with Self-Supervised Preference Data](https://aclanthology.org/2025.acl-long.1165/)** — Sun et al. · *ACL 2025*<br>SSPO: constructs translation preference data self-supervised for iterative DPO; a 14B LLM matches or beats GPT-4o on FLORES and multi-domain tests; releases an augmented MQM dataset.
- `2025` **[End-to-End Multilingual Automatic Dubbing via Duration-based Translation with Large Language Models](https://aclanthology.org/2025.emnlp-demos.37/)** — Won et al. · *EMNLP 2025 (demo)*<br>Duration-based Translation: LLM predicts phoneme counts from source speech duration and adjusts translation length for dubbing, with up to 24% relative speech-overlap gains on English, Spanish and Korean.
- `2025` **[Detect, Disambiguate, and Translate: On-Demand Visual Reasoning for Multimodal Machine Translation with Large Vision-Language Models](https://aclanthology.org/2025.naacl-long.74/)** — Liu et al. · *NAACL 2025*<br>DeDiT: reasoning-based multimodal MT framework that uses large vision-language models to detect ambiguity and apply visual reasoning only when needed, outperforming prior systems on Multi30K and CoMMuTE.
- `2025` **[Can Large Language Models Translate Unseen Languages in Underrepresented Scripts?](https://aclanthology.org/2025.emnlp-main.1179/)** — Lin et al. · *EMNLP 2025*<br>Lotus: benchmark for LLM translation of Mongolian in traditional script and Yi; linguistic resources raise automatic scores, but LLMs remain limited on these unseen, underrepresented-script languages.
- `2025` **[Can LLMs Translate Cultural Nuance in Dialects? A Case Study on Lebanese Arabic](https://aclanthology.org/2025.abjadnlp-1.13/)** — Yakhni & Chehab · *ABJADNLP 2025 (workshop)*<br>Introduces a culturally rich Lebanese-English dataset from the Language Wave podcast; LLMs beat NMT at preserving cultural nuance on authentic content, and xCOMET correlates strongly with human judgment.
- `2025` **[CaMMT: Benchmarking Culturally Aware Multimodal Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2025.findings-emnlp.1220/)** — Villa-Cueva et al. · *Findings of EMNLP 2025*<br>CaMMT: over 5,800 image-caption triples in English and regional languages for culturally aware multimodal translation; images improve VLM translation of culture-specific items, disambiguation and gender marking.
- `2025` **[Alligators All Around: Mitigating Lexical Confusion in Low-resource Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2025.naacl-short.18/)** — Nielsen et al. · *NAACL 2025 (short)*<br>Measures within-domain lexical confusion in low-resource MT across 122 languages and mitigates it with LLM post-editing using the full GATITOS lexicon as a long-context prompt.
- `2024-12` **[DRT: Deep Reasoning Translation via Long Chain-of-Thought](https://aclanthology.org/2025.findings-acl.351/)** — Wang et al. · *Findings of ACL 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.17498)]<br>DRT: trains Qwen2.5 and Llama-3.1 on tens of thousands of long-thought MT examples for literary similes and metaphors, synthesized by a translator-advisor-evaluator multi-agent loop.
- `2024-11` **[Using Language Models to Disambiguate Lexical Choices in Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.278/)** — Barua et al. · *EMNLP 2024* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.05781)]<br>DTAiLS: 1,377 sentence pairs in nine languages showing cross-lingual concept variation; GPT-4 reaches 67 to 85% lexical selection accuracy, and LLM-generated lexical rules boost weaker models.
- `2024-11` **[Transcending Language Boundaries: Harnessing LLMs for Low-Resource Language Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.11295)** — Shu et al.<br>Proposes keyword-focused retrieval of examples to improve LLM translation from English into Cherokee, Tibetan and Manchu, where zero-shot GPT-4o and LLaMA 3.1 405B struggle.
- `2024-10` **[X-ALMA: Plug & Play Modules and Adaptive Rejection for Quality Translation at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.03115)** — Xu et al. · *ICLR 2024*<br>X-ALMA: translation model with plug-and-play language-specific modules and ARPO preference optimization covering 50 languages; beats Aya-101 and Aya-23 in every FLORES-200 and WMT'23 direction by COMET-22.
- `2024-10` **[Towards Cross-Cultural Machine Translation with Retrieval-Augmented Generation from Multilingual Knowledge Graphs](https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.914/)** — Conia et al. · *EMNLP 2024* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.14057)]<br>XC-Translate: manually created MT benchmark of culturally nuanced entity names; KG-MT, retrieving from a multilingual knowledge graph, improves 129% and 62% relative over NLLB-200 and GPT-4.
- `2024-10` **[Modeling User Preferences with Automatic Metrics: Creating a High-Quality Preference Dataset for Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.803/)** — Agrawal et al. · *EMNLP 2024* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.07779)]<br>MT-Pref: 18k translation preference instances over 18 language directions, built from linguist judgments and automatic metrics; aligning Tower models on it improves WMT23 and FLORES quality.
- `2024-10` **[Large Language Model for Multi-Domain Translation: Benchmarking and Domain CoT Fine-tuning](https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.328/)** — Hu et al. · *Findings of EMNLP 2024* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.02631)]<br>Builds a multi-domain MT benchmark (25 German-English and 22 Chinese-English test sets, 15 domains) and proposes domain CoT fine-tuning where the LLM infers the source domain as a hint.
- `2024-10` **[LLM-based Translation Inference with Iterative Bilingual Understanding](https://aclanthology.org/2025.findings-acl.867/)** — Chen et al. · *Findings of ACL 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.12543)]<br>IBUT: LLM translation method that generates source- and target-side contextual understanding and refines it iteratively with cross-lingual feedback, outperforming strong baselines across news, commonsense and cultural benchmarks.
- `2024-10` **[GrammaMT: Improving Machine Translation with Grammar-Informed In-Context Learning](https://aclanthology.org/2025.acl-long.1447/)** — Ramos et al. · *ACL 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.18702)]<br>GrammaMT: training-free prompting with interlinear glossed text (gloss-shot, chain-gloss, model-gloss) that improves LLM translation for low- to high-resource languages, including endangered languages.
- `2024-10` **[ELDA: Enhancing Multi-Modal Machine Translation via Large Language Model-Driven Data Augmentation](https://doi.org/10.1109/MLNLP63328.2024.10800404)** — Wang et al. · *ICML 2024*<br>ELDA: prompts GPT-3.5 with multi-attribute in-context examples to augment multimodal MT training data, improving leading MMT methods by up to 3.26 BLEU and 2.64 METEOR.
- `2024-10` **[DelTA: An Online Document-Level Translation Agent Based on Multi-Level Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.08143)** — Wang et al. · *ICLR 2024*<br>DelTA: document-level LLM translation agent with multi-level memory (proper nouns, bilingual summary, long- and short-term memory), raising consistency by up to 4.58 points and COMET by up to 3.16.
- `2024-10` **[Boosting LLM Translation Skills without General Ability Loss via Rationale Distillation](https://aclanthology.org/2025.findings-acl.632/)** — Wu et al. · *Findings of ACL 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.13944)]<br>RaDis: fine-tunes LLMs for MT jointly on reference translations and self-generated rationales replayed to prevent forgetting, improving translation while preserving general abilities.
- `2024-09` **[Mufu: Multilingual Fused Learning for Low-Resource Translation with LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.13949)** — Lim et al. · *ICLR 2025*<br>Mufu: turns low-resource translation into post-editing by prompting with multilingual auxiliary candidates; LLMs fine-tuned this way beat NLLB 1.3B distilled in 64% of low- and very-low-resource pairs.
- `2024-09` **[How Much Data is Enough Data? Fine-Tuning Large Language Models for In-House Translation: Performance Evaluation Across Multiple Dataset Sizes](https://aclanthology.org/2024.amta-research.20/)** — Vieira et al. · *AMTA 2024 (workshop)* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.03454)]<br>Fine-tunes Llama 3 8B Instruct on organisational translation memories (1k to 100k+ segments) in five directions; quality improves with data size, but 1k-2k examples degrade it below the baseline.
- `2024-08` **[SCOI: Syntax-augmented Coverage-based In-context Example Selection for Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.555/)** — Tang et al. · *EMNLP 2024* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.04872)]<br>SCOI: selects in-context MT examples by combining syntactic and lexical coverage, giving the highest average COMET among learning-free methods across six directions with two multilingual LLMs.
- `2024-08` **[Quality or Quantity? On Data Scale and Diversity in Adapting Large Language Models for Low-Resource Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2024.wmt-1.128/)** — Iyer et al. · *WMT 2024 (workshop)* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.12780)]<br>Shows that adapting LLMs for low-resource MT needs parallel data in pretraining and SFT, while SFT diversity tends to cause interference, across Indigenous American and North-East Indian languages.
- `2024-08` **[In-Context Example Selection via Similarity Search Improves Low-Resource Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2025.findings-naacl.68/)** — Zebaze et al. · *Findings of NAACL 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.00397)]<br>Compares in-context example retrieval for LLM MT from English into French, German, Swahili and Wolof; embedding similarity helps, especially for low-resource directions; proposes an adapted COMET protocol.
- `2024-08` **[FLEURS-ASL: Including American Sign Language in Massively Multilingual Multitask Evaluation](https://aclanthology.org/2025.naacl-long.314/)** — Tanzer · *NAACL 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.13585)]<br>FLEURS-ASL: extends FLORES and FLEURS with American Sign Language video, supporting translation between ASL and 200 text or 102 speech languages; frontier multimodal models show virtually no ASL understanding.
- `2024-08` **[Benchmarking LLMs for Translating Classical Chinese Poetry: Evaluating Adequacy, Fluency, and Elegance](https://aclanthology.org/2025.emnlp-main.1678/)** — Chen et al. · *EMNLP 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.09945)]<br>PoetMT: classical Chinese poetry translation benchmark with a GPT-4-based metric; LLMs fall short, and retrieval-augmented translation with poetry knowledge outperforms all compared methods.
- `2024-07` **[Translate-and-Revise: Boosting Large Language Models for Constrained Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2024.ccl-1.82/)** — Huang et al. · *CCL 2024 (workshop)* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.13164)]<br>Adds a revision step prompting LLMs about unmet constraints, giving 15% higher constraint-based accuracy than standard LLMs on four lexical and structural constrained translation tasks.
- `2024-07` **[Toucan: Many-to-Many Translation for 150 African Language Pairs](https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-acl.781/)** — Elmadany et al. · *Findings of ACL 2024* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.04796)]<br>Toucan: Afrocentric MT model for 156 African language pairs built on Cheetah-1.2B and Cheetah-3.7B, with the AfroLingu-MT benchmark and spBLEU-1K metric covering 1K languages.
- `2024-07` **[Improving LLM Abilities in Idiomatic Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2025.loreslm-1.13/)** — Donthi et al. · *LORESLM 2025 (workshop)* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.03518)]<br>Extends IdiomKB to find corresponding target-language idioms for LLM translation; in human evaluation of English-Chinese with GPT-4o, cosine-similarity idiom lookup beats the other methods.
- `2024-07` **[GPT-4 vs. Human Translators: A Comprehensive Evaluation of Translation Quality Across Languages, Domains, and Expertise Levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.03658)** — Yan et al.<br>Human error annotation finds GPT-4 translations comparable to junior translators but behind medium and senior ones, weakening toward resource-poor directions and prone to literal translation.
- `2024-06` **[What is the Best Way for ChatGPT to Translate Poetry?](https://aclanthology.org/2024.acl-long.756/)** — Wang et al. · *ACL 2024* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.03450)]<br>Studies ChatGPT English-Chinese poetry translation and proposes EAPMT, guiding translation with monolingual poetry explanations, which poets and GPT-4 rate above ChatGPT baselines and online systems.
- `2024-06` **[TasTe: Teaching Large Language Models to Translate through Self-Reflection](https://aclanthology.org/2024.acl-long.333/)** — Wang et al. · *ACL 2024* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.08434)]<br>TasTe: two-stage self-reflection where LLMs draft and self-assess translations, then refine them, improving results in four WMT22 directions.
- `2024-06` **[Shortcomings of LLMs for Low-Resource Translation: Retrieval and Understanding Are Both the Problem](https://aclanthology.org/2024.wmt-1.125/)** — Court & Elsner · *WMT 2024 (workshop)* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.15625)]<br>Studies in-context Southern Quechua-to-Spanish translation with retrieved morpheme translations, grammar descriptions and corpus examples; even small LLMs use minimally sufficient linguistic context zero-shot.
- `2024-06` **[Paying More Attention to Source Context: Mitigating Unfaithful Translations from Large Language Model](https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-acl.821/)** — Zhang et al. · *Findings of ACL 2024* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.07036)]<br>Finds LLM translators favor previously generated tokens over source tokens and reduces unfaithful translation by adjusting source attention and limiting target-prefix influence in prompting and instruction tuning.
- `2024-06` **[Ladder: A Model-Agnostic Framework Boosting LLM-based Machine Translation to the Next Level](https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.860/)** — Feng et al. · *EMNLP 2024* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.15741)]<br>MT-Ladder: model-agnostic refiner trained on pseudo-refinement triplets with easy-to-hard fine-tuning; MT-Ladder-2B raises BigTranslate-13B by +6.91 BLEU and +3.52 COMET for XX→En.
- `2024-06` **[LLMs Are Zero-Shot Context-Aware Simultaneous Translators](https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.69/)** — Koshkin et al. · *EMNLP 2024* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.13476)]<br>Shows open-source LLMs match or beat some simultaneous MT baselines zero-shot, with injected background information bringing further gains on technical subject-matter.
- `2024-06` **[Efficiently Exploring Large Language Models for Document-Level Machine Translation with In-context Learning](https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-acl.646/)** — Cui et al. · *Findings of ACL 2024* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.07081)]<br>Context-Aware Prompting (CAP): selects relevant context sentences, summarizes them and retrieves similar demonstrations, making LLM document-level translation more coherent, notably for zero pronouns and literary text.
- `2024-06` **[DUAL-REFLECT: Enhancing Large Language Models for Reflective Translation through Dual Learning Feedback Mechanisms](https://aclanthology.org/2024.acl-short.64/)** — Chen et al. · *ACL 2024 (short)* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.07232)]<br>DUAL-REFLECT: uses dual-learning feedback from translation tasks to improve LLM self-reflective translation, with gains especially on low-resource language pairs.
- `2024-05` **[Word Alignment as Preference for Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.188/)** — Wu et al. · *EMNLP 2024* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.09223)]<br>Builds chosen/rejected translation pairs by word alignment and applies DPO to an LLM-based MT model to reduce hallucination and omission, evaluated on hard instances with GPT-4.
- `2024-05` **[Towards Robust In-Context Learning for Machine Translation with Large Language Models](https://aclanthology.org/2024.lrec-main.1444/)** — Zhu et al. · *LREC-COLING 2024*<br>Selects in-context MT demonstrations with sentence-level margin scores and word-level embedding weighting to reduce noise from retrieved examples in LLM translation.
- `2024-05` **[The Fine-Tuning Paradox: Boosting Translation Quality Without Sacrificing LLM Abilities](https://aclanthology.org/2024.acl-long.336/)** — Stap et al. · *ACL 2024* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.20089)]<br>Shows fine-tuning LLaMA and Falcon (7B-65B) on parallel data improves translation but degrades formality steering, few-shot technical translation and document-level translation; adding monolingual data preserves them.
- `2024-05` **[Teaching Large Language Models to Translate on Low-resource Languages with Textbook Prompting](https://aclanthology.org/2024.lrec-main.1362/)** — Guo et al. · *LREC-COLING 2024*<br>TALENT: has LLMs learn from a constructed textbook and syntax patterns before translating; improves ChatGPT and BLOOMZ by 14.8% over zero-shot on 112 low-resource FLORES-200 languages.
- `2024-05` **[TAeKD: Teacher Assistant Enhanced Knowledge Distillation for Closed-Source Multilingual Neural Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2024.lrec-main.1350/)** — Lv et al. · *LREC-COLING 2024*<br>TAeKD: fuses outputs of several closed-source LLM-based multilingual MT models into soft labels and training data to distill them into smaller models, with quality assessment learning.
- `2024-05` **[Simultaneous Masking, Not Prompting Optimization: A Paradigm Shift in Fine-tuning LLMs for Simultaneous Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.1017/)** — Raffel et al. · *EMNLP 2024* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.10443)]<br>SimulMask: fine-tunes LLMs for simultaneous translation with attention masks modeling a decision policy; on Falcon and IWSLT 2017 it beats prompt-optimization methods on five pairs at lower cost.
- `2024-05` **[Relay Decoding: Concatenating Large Language Models for Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.02933)** — Fu et al.<br>Relay Decoding: concatenates two LLMs that separately support the source and target languages via a mapping layer trained on limited parallel data, evaluated on Multi30k and WikiMatrix.
- `2024-05` **[LLM-Assisted Rule Based Machine Translation for Low/No-Resource Languages](https://aclanthology.org/2024.americasnlp-1.9/)** — Coleman et al. · *AMERICASNLP 2024 (workshop)* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.08997)]<br>LLM-RBMT: LLM-assisted rule-based MT for no-resource languages, used to build an education-oriented translator for critically endangered Owens Valley Paiute.
- `2024-05` **[How Multilingual Are Large Language Models Fine-Tuned for Translation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.20512)** — Richburg & Carpuat · *COLM 2024*<br>Evaluates Tower LLMs on 132 FLORES-200 translation tasks; translation fine-tuning improves quality even for zero-shot languages on average, but unevenly across language pairs.
- `2024-05` **[G-DIG: Towards Gradient-based DIverse and hiGh-quality Instruction Data Selection for Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2024.acl-long.821/)** — Pan et al. · *ACL 2024* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.12915)]<br>G-DIG: selects high-quality, diverse MT instruction data with influence functions and gradient clustering, improving LLM translation on WMT22 and FLORES.
- `2024-04` **[Low-Resource Machine Translation through Retrieval-Augmented LLM Prompting: A Study on the Mambai Language](https://aclanthology.org/2024.eurali-1.1/)** — Merx et al. · *EURALI 2024 (workshop)* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.04809)]<br>Prompts Llama 2 70B, Mixtral 8x7B and GPT-4 for English-Mambai MT with retrieved sentences and dictionary entries, which improve quality; releases an initial Mambai corpus.
- `2024-04` **[Guiding Large Language Models to Post-Edit Machine Translation with Error Annotations](https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-naacl.265/)** — Ki & Carpuat · *Findings of NAACL 2024* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.07851)]<br>Prompts and fine-tunes LLaMA-2 to post-edit MT using MQM error feedback on Chinese-English, English-German and English-Russian; post-editing improves TER, BLEU and COMET.
- `2024-04` **[From LLM to NMT: Advancing Low-Resource Machine Translation with Claude](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.13813)** — Enis & Hopkins<br>Shows Claude 3 Opus has stronger MT competence than other LLMs and high resource efficiency, and distills it into NMT models that advance Yoruba-English translation.
- `2024-04` **[Fine-Tuning Large Language Models to Translate: Will a Touch of Noisy Data in Misaligned Languages Suffice?](https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.24/)** — Zhu et al. · *EMNLP 2024* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.14122)]<br>LLMs translate well after fine-tuning on as few as 32 parallel sentences and one direction transfers to others, but English-only targets cause task misinterpretation for non-English outputs.
- `2024-04` **[A Preference-driven Paradigm for Enhanced Translation with Large Language Models](https://aclanthology.org/2024.naacl-long.186/)** — Zhu et al. · *NAACL 2024* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.11288)]<br>Proposes Plackett-Luce preference training for LLM translation and the MAPLE dataset of multiple translations of varying quality per source, overcoming the plateau of SFT on parallel data.
- `2024-03` **[Prompting ChatGPT for Translation: A Comparative Analysis of Translation Brief and Persona Prompts](https://aclanthology.org/2024.eamt-1.27/)** — He · *EAMT 2024 (workshop)* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.00127)]<br>Finds that translation-brief and translator/author persona prompts have limited effect on ChatGPT translation quality, despite their value in human-to-human translation.
- `2024-03` **[MT-PATCHER: Selective and Extendable Knowledge Distillation from Large Language Models for Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2024.naacl-long.358/)** — Li et al. · *NAACL 2024* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.09522)]<br>MT-Patcher: distills LLM translation knowledge selectively into MT models by correcting only student errors and synthesizing diverse contexts; about 10% of examples match traditional distillation.
- `2024-03` **[A Novel Paradigm Boosting Translation Capabilities of Large Language Models](https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-naacl.42/)** — Guo et al. · *Findings of NAACL 2024* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.11430)]<br>Three-stage recipe for LLM translation: secondary pretraining on monolingual data, continual pretraining on interlinear-format documents, and source-language-consistent SFT; improves Llama2 and Chinese-Llama2 translation.
- `2024-02` **[TransLLaMa: LLM-based Simultaneous Translation System](https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.27/)** — Koshkin et al. · *Findings of EMNLP 2024* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.04636)]<br>TransLLaMa: fine-tunes an open LLM on causally aligned sentence pairs to emit a wait token for simultaneous translation, reaching baseline-level BLEU on English-German and English-Russian.
- `2024-02` **[TEaR: Improving LLM-based Machine Translation with Systematic Self-Refinement](https://aclanthology.org/2025.findings-naacl.218/)** — Feng et al. · *Findings of NAACL 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.16379)]<br>TEaR: Translate, Estimate and Refine framework in which LLMs self-refine translations using estimated errors, improving quality across high- and low-resource and non-English-centric directions.
- `2024-02` **[SiLLM: Large Language Models for Simultaneous Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.13036)** — Guo et al.<br>SiLLM: simultaneous MT pairing a conventional policy agent with an LLM translation agent, using a word-level policy adapted for LLMs and little fine-tuning data.
- `2024-02` **[Self-Augmented In-Context Learning for Unsupervised Word Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2024.acl-short.67/)** — Li et al. · *ACL 2024 (short)* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.10024)]<br>SAIL: iteratively induces high-confidence word translation pairs from an LLM and reuses them as in-context examples, beating zero-shot prompting and mapping-based baselines on two unsupervised BLI benchmarks.
- `2024-02` **[Predicting Machine Translation Performance on Low-Resource Languages: The Role of Domain Similarity](https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-eacl.100/)** — Khiu et al. · *Findings of EACL 2024* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.02633)]<br>Predicts fine-tuned multilingual LLM translation performance for low-resource languages from corpus size, domain similarity and language similarity, finding domain similarity has the most critical impact.
- `2024-02` **[Large Language Models "Ad Referendum": How Good Are They at Machine Translation in the Legal Domain?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.07681)** — Briva-Iglesias et al. · *MonTI : monographs in translation and interpreting 2024*<br>Compares two LLMs with Google Translate on legal MT across four language pairs; Google Translate wins on automatic metrics, but professional translators rate GPT-4 comparably or slightly better.
- `2024-02` **[GenTranslate: Large Language Models are Generative Multilingual Speech and Machine Translators](https://aclanthology.org/2024.acl-long.5/)** — Hu et al. · *ACL 2024* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.06894)]<br>GenTranslate: LLMs fuse N-best translation hypotheses into a better output for speech and text translation; releases HypoTranslate, 592K hypothesis-translation pairs in 11 languages.
- `2024-02` **[Fine-tuning Large Language Models for Domain-specific Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.15061)** — Zheng et al.<br>DragFT: fine-tunes LLMs for domain-specific MT with dictionary-enhanced prompting, RAG-based few-shot example selection and in-domain few-shot fine-tuning, outperforming GPT-3.5 and GPT-4o on three domains.
- `2024-02` **[DeMPT: Decoding-enhanced Multi-phase Prompt Tuning for Making LLMs Be Better Context-aware Translators](https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.1131/)** — Lyu et al. · *EMNLP 2024* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.15200)]<br>DeMPT: multi-phase prompt tuning that separately models inter- and intra-sentence context for context-aware LLM translation, outperforming plain concatenation and improving discourse modeling.
- `2024-02` **[Advancing Translation Preference Modeling with RLHF: A Step Towards Cost-Effective Solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.11525)** — Xu et al.<br>Trains translation reward models to distinguish human from machine translations; RLHF with them improves translation quality and also benefits translation directions not trained with RLHF.
- `2024-02` **[A Prompt Response to the Demand for Automatic Gender-Neutral Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2024.eacl-short.23/)** — Savoldi et al. · *EACL 2024 (short)* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.06041)]<br>Compares MT systems with prompted GPT-4 for gender-neutral translation through manual analyses, showing MT limitations and the potential and challenges of prompting for neutrality.
- `2024-01` **[Tuning LLMs with Contrastive Alignment Instructions for Machine Translation in Unseen, Low-resource Languages](https://aclanthology.org/2024.loresmt-1.1/)** — Mao & Yu · *LORESMT 2024 (workshop)* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.05811)]<br>AlignInstruct: contrastive alignment instructions using a word-alignment discriminator; fine-tuning BLOOMZ on up to 24 unseen languages improves translation across 48 English-centric directions and 30 zero-shot directions.
- `2024-01` **[Towards Boosting Many-to-Many Multilingual Machine Translation with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.05861)** — Gao et al.<br>Shows prompt strategies in translation finetuning are crucial for zero-shot directions and adapts CrossConST as XConST consistency regularization, improving many-to-many translation on ALMA, Tower and LLaMA-2.
- `2024-01` **[Salute the Classic: Revisiting Challenges of Machine Translation in the Age of Large Language Models](https://aclanthology.org/2025.tacl-1.4/)** — Pang et al. · *TACL 2025* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.08350)]<br>Revisits six classic NMT challenges with LLMs: parallel-data reliance and long-sentence translation improve, domain mismatch and rare words persist; adds efficiency, low-resource and evaluation challenges.
- `2024-01` **[POMP: Probability-driven Meta-graph Prompter for LLMs in Low-resource Unsupervised Neural Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2024.acl-long.537/)** — Pan et al. · *ACL 2024* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.05596)]<br>POMP: dynamic graph of auxiliary languages with BLEURT-driven path probabilities to improve LLM unsupervised translation, yielding significant gains for three low-resource languages.
- `2024-01` **[Machine Translation with Large Language Models: Prompt Engineering for Persian, English, and Russian Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.08429)** — Pourkamali & Sharifi<br>Compares n-shot and tailored prompting for LLM translation among Persian, English and Russian, and categorizes LLM translation errors by linguistic metrics.
- `2024-01` **[Enhancing Document-level Translation of Large Language Model via Translation Mixed-instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.08088)** — Li et al.<br>Fine-tunes Llama-2 7B and 13B on mixed sentence- and document-level translation instructions, keeping quality up to 2048-token documents across 10 language pairs and mitigating sentence-level coverage gaps.
- `2024-01` **[Contrastive Preference Optimization: Pushing the Boundaries of LLM Performance in Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.08417)** — Xu et al. · *ICML 2024*<br>CPO: contrastive preference optimization training LLMs to avoid adequate-but-imperfect translations; applied to ALMA with 22K parallel sentences, ALMA-R matches or exceeds WMT winners and GPT-4.
- `2024-01` **[An Empirical Study of In-context Learning in LLMs for Machine Translation](https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-acl.440/)** — Chitale et al. · *Findings of ACL 2024* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.12097)]<br>Studies in-context learning for LLM MT, finding it example-driven rather than instruction-driven, target-side demonstration quality matters most, and related-task examples with the same target distribution suffice.
- `2024-01` **[Aligning Translation-Specific Understanding to General Understanding in Large Language Models](https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.289/)** — Huang et al. · *EMNLP 2024* · [[arXiv](https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.05072)]<br>DUAT: adds LLM-generated cross-lingual interpretations of difficult words to guide translation, evaluated on the Challenge-WMT benchmark, improving COMET by up to +3.85 and reducing literalness by 25% to 51%.
